### Tool call: browser_evaluate
- Args
```json
{
  "function": "async () => {\n  // 1つ目のキャストのチェックボックスを現在色(yellow=壁側)でトグル\n  const cb = document.querySelector('.host-panel .cast-checkbox');\n  if (!cb) return { error: 'no cast checkbox found' };\n  cb.click();\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));\n  const badge = document.querySelector('.selecting-badges .selecting-badge');\n  const pickerVisible = getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.color-picker')).display;\n  const resetVisible = getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.reset-btn')).display;\n  // sw 用 Cache Storage の中身\n  let swCache = null;\n  try {\n    const c = await caches.open('hm-store-settings');\n    const res = await c.match('/__store-settings');\n    swCache = res ? await res.json() : 'not written';\n  } catch (e) { swCache = 'caches error: ' + e.message; }\n  return { badgeText: badge ? badge.textContent : null, pickerVisible, resetVisible, swCache };\n}"
}
```

{
  "function": "() => {\n  const rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('.item-row, .sortable-item, [class*=\"item\"]')];\n  // 紫月 の行を名前で探す\n  const all = [...document.querySelectorAll('*')].filter(el => el.children.length===0 && el.textContent.trim()==='紫月');\n  const info = all.map(el => {\n    const row = el.closest('div');\n    return row ? row.parentElement.outerHTML.slice(0,200) : 'no-row';\n  });\n  return info;\n}"
}
```

{
  "function": "async () => {\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1500)); // アップロード＋upsert待ち\n  const URL='https://ktvenszzbejbioafiilc.supabase.co';\n  const ANON='eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJzdXBhYmFzZSIsInJlZiI6Imt0dmVuc3p6YmVqYmlvYWZpaWxjIiwicm9sZSI6ImFub24iLCJpYXQiOjE3Nzc3MjgxNzYsImV4cCI6MjA5MzMwNDE3Nn0.0g1c2vQC_sD3eStYx29GDGyIY_UH5HBBPnJhXuAOiJs';\n  const id='item-1776255528163-sjg9qm';\n  const r = await fetch(`${URL}/rest/v1/panels?select=id,name,extra_images&id=eq.${id}`, { headers: { apikey: ANON, Authorization: 'Bearer '+ANON }});\n  const cloud = await r.json();\n  const key = cloud[0]?.extra_images?.[0]?.key;\n  let storageOk = null;\n  if (key) {\n    const sr = await fetch(`${URL}/storage/v1/object/public/panel-images/${key}.jpg`, { method:'GET' });\n    storageOk = sr.status;\n  }\n  return { cloudExtra: cloud[0]?.extra_images, storageStatus: storageOk };\n}"
}
```
